CCLAUS         UNTIL SUB > RSP06-IFR-ENTRY-CTR OF RSP06-RGC 
CCLAUS         MOVE ZEROES TO RSP06-IB-IFR-FEE-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC(SUB)
CCLAUS     END-PERFORM. 
CCLAUS  
CCLAUS     COMPUTE RSP06-IB-SAIL-GST-PCT OF RSP06-RGC = 
CCLAUS           ((RSP06-IBR-12NM-PCT OF RSP06-RGC *
CCLAUS                (1 + RSP06-IBR-GST-PCT OF RSP06-RGC)) /   
CCLAUS            (1 + (RSP06-IBR-12NM-PCT OF RSP06-RGC *   
CCLAUS               RSP06-IBR-GST-PCT OF RSP06-RGC))). 
CCLAUS  
CCLAUS     COMPUTE RSP06-IB-EFF-INCL-GST-PCT OF RSP06-RGC = 
CCLAUS            (RSP06-IBR-GST-PCT OF RSP06-RGC * RSP06-IBR-12NM-PCT  
CCLAUS             OF RSP06-RGC) / (1 + (RSP06-IBR-GST-PCT OF RSP06-RGC 
CCLAUS                          * RSP06-IBR-12NM-PCT OF RSP06-RGC)).
CCLAUS  
CCLAUS     COMPUTE RSP06-IB-EFF-EXCL-GST-PCT OF RSP06-RGC = 
CCLAUS             RSP06-IBR-12NM-PCT OF RSP06-RGC * RSP06-IBR-GST-PCT  
CCLAUS                                                OF RSP06-RGC .
CCLAUS  
CCLAUS     COMPUTE RSP06-IB-EFF-INCL-TRNS-GST-PCT OF RSP06-RGC =
CCLAUS             RSP06-IBR-GST-PCT OF RSP06-RGC / 
CCLAUS            (1 + RSP06-IBR-GST-PCT OF RSP06-RGC). 
CCLAUS  
CCLAUS     MOVE RSP06-IBR-CUSTOMER-NUM  OF RSP06-RGC
CCLAUS                                TO AAR-NUM.   
CCLAUS     MOVE 'CM'                  TO AAR-TYPE.

CCLAUS            ON ERROR GO TO 0250-AGENT-ERROR.  
CCLAUS     FETCH OWNER WITHIN AGENCY-ASSOCIATION-SET SET
CCLAUS            ON ERROR GO TO 0250-AGENT-ERROR.  
CCLAUS     GO TO 0250-CONT. 
CCLAUS

CCLAUS

CCLAUS        MOVE ZEROES TO WS-GST-PCT 
CCLAUS      ELSE
CCLAUS         GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR
CCLAUS      END-IF. 
CCLAUS  
CCLAUS 0250-CONT.

CCLAUS        MOVE ZEROES TO RSP06-IB-EFF-EXCL-GST-PCT OF RSP06-RGC 
CCLAUS        MOVE ZEROES TO WS-GST-PCT 
CCLAUS     END-IF.

PAXOBC*       WHEN 'L' WHEN 'M' WHEN '4'
PAXOBC*             MOVE '4'  TO PAXOBC-CALCAREA (7:1)

PAXOBC*             MOVE '5'  TO PAXOBC-CALCAREA (7:1)

PAXOBC*             MOVE '6'  TO PAXOBC-CALCAREA (7:1)  
PAXOBC*       WHEN 'S' WHEN 'T' WHEN '7'
PAXOBC*             MOVE '7'  TO PAXOBC-CALCAREA (7:1)  
PAXOBC*       WHEN 'V' WHEN 'W' WHEN '8'
PAXOBC*             MOVE '8'  TO PAXOBC-CALCAREA (7:1)

PAXOBC*             MOVE '9'  TO PAXOBC-CALCAREA (7:1)

           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB3 FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL
             WS-SUB3 > RSP06-ENTITY-CNT OF RSP06-RGC(WS-SUB2)   
              IF RSP06-ENTITY-TYPE OF RSP06-RGC(WS-SUB2 WS-SUB3) = 'RTE'
                 IF WS-SUB3 NOT = 1 
                    MOVE RSP06-OFFER-COMPONENT-TABLE OF RSP06-RGC   
                    (WS-SUB2 WS-SUB3) TO WS-RSP06-OFF-COMP  
                    PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB4 FROM WS-SUB3 BY -1 UNTIL
                           WS-SUB4 <= 1 
                       MOVE RSP06-OFFER-COMPONENT-TABLE OF RSP06-RGC
                                        (WS-SUB2 WS-SUB4 - 1)   
                       TO   RSP06-OFFER-COMPONENT-TABLE OF RSP06-RGC
                                        (WS-SUB2 WS-SUB4)   
                    END-PERFORM 
                    MOVE WS-RSP06-OFF-COMP TO   
                    RSP06-OFFER-COMPONENT-TABLE OF RSP06-RGC(WS-SUB2 1) 
                 END-IF 
                 MOVE RSP06-ENTITY-CNT OF RSP06-RGC(WS-SUB2) TO WS-SUB3 
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 
           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB3 FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL
             WS-SUB3 > RSP06-ENTITY-CNT OF RSP06-RGC(WS-SUB2)   
              IF RSP06-ENTITY-TYPE OF RSP06-RGC(WS-SUB2 WS-SUB3)
                 = 'ADB' AND
                 RSP06-ENTITY-CODE OF RSP06-RGC(WS-SUB2 WS-SUB3)(1:1)   
                 = 'E'  
                 IF WS-SUB3 NOT = 2 
                    MOVE RSP06-OFFER-COMPONENT-TABLE OF RSP06-RGC   
                    (WS-SUB2 WS-SUB3) TO WS-RSP06-OFF-COMP  
                    PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB4 FROM WS-SUB3 BY -1 UNTIL
                           WS-SUB4 <= 2 
                       MOVE RSP06-OFFER-COMPONENT-TABLE OF RSP06-RGC
                                        (WS-SUB2 WS-SUB4 - 1)   
                       TO   RSP06-OFFER-COMPONENT-TABLE OF RSP06-RGC
                                        (WS-SUB2 WS-SUB4)   
                    END-PERFORM 
                    MOVE WS-RSP06-OFF-COMP TO   
                    RSP06-OFFER-COMPONENT-TABLE OF RSP06-RGC(WS-SUB2 2) 
                 END-IF 
                 MOVE RSP06-ENTITY-CNT OF RSP06-RGC(WS-SUB2) TO WS-SUB3 
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM.

PPC470            OR CATEGORY_CODE = :WS-CPA-CATEGORY-CODE) 
PPC470        FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
PPC470     END-EXEC.

PPC470        GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
PPC470     ELSE

PPC470           GO TO 9200-EDIT-ERROR-MSG  
PPC470        END-IF
PPC470     END-IF.

CCLAUS     COMPUTE RSP06-IBR-TOTAL-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC =   
CCLAUS             RSP06-IB-ADDON-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC         +
CCLAUS             RSP06-IB-ADMIN-FEE-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC     +
CCLAUS             RSP06-IB-COURTESY-ORD-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC  -
CCLAUS             RSP06-IB-CREDIT-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC        +
CCLAUS             RSP06-IB-CRUISE-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC        +
CCLAUS             RSP06-IB-DAMAGE-DEP-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC    +
CCLAUS             RSP06-IB-INS-PREMIUM-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC   +
CCLAUS             RSP06-IB-MISC-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC          +
CCLAUS             RSP06-IB-NSF-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC           +
CCLAUS             RSP06-IB-PACKAGE-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC       +
CCLAUS             RSP06-IB-PENALTY-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC       +
CCLAUS             RSP06-IB-PP-GRATS-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC      +
CCLAUS             RSP06-IB-PTCHG-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC         +
CCLAUS             RSP06-IB-TOTAL-FEES-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC    +
CCLAUS             RSP06-IB-TRANSFER-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC.  
CCLAUS  
CCLAUS     COMPUTE RSP06-IBR-COMM-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC =
CCLAUS             RSP06-IB-INS-COMM-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC     + 
CCLAUS             RSP06-IBR-CRUO-COMM-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC   + 
CCLAUS             RSP06-IBR-TRANS-COMM-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC  + 
CCLAUS             RSP06-IBR-PEN-COMM-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC    + 
CCLAUS             RSP06-IBR-PKG-COMM-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC    + 
CCLAUS             RSP06-IBR-ADDON-COMM-GST-AMT OF RSP06-RGC.   
CCLAUS

108267 5400-PROCESS-MAR.
219528*    IF MAR-STATUS-CODE NOT = 'A' 
219528     IF IRP64-STATUS-CODE NOT = 'A'   
108267        GO TO 5350-PROCESS-NEXT-MAR.  
108267  
219528*    EVALUATE MAR-TYPE
219528     EVALUATE IRP64-TYPE  
108267        WHEN 'P'  
108267             COMPUTE RSP06-IBR-PENALTY-AMT OF RSP06-RGC ROUNDED = 
108267                     RSP06-IBR-PENALTY-AMT OF RSP06-RGC + 
219528*                    (MAR-ACTUAL-QUANTITY * MAR-ACTUAL-AMOUNT /   
219528*                     MAR-PRC-XCHG-RATE)  
219528                     (IRP64-ACTUAL-QTY * IRP64-ACTUAL-AMOUNT /
219528                      IRP64-PRC-XCHG-RATE)
108267             COMPUTE RSP06-IBR-PENALTY-COMM-AMT OF RSP06-RGC  
                           ROUNDED =
108267                     RSP06-IBR-PENALTY-COMM-AMT OF RSP06-RGC +
219528*                    (MAR-COMM-AMT / MAR-PRC-XCHG-RATE)   
219528                      (IRP64-COMM-AMT / IRP64-PRC-XCHG-RATE)  
219528*            IF MAR-PAX-SEQ-NUM <= ZERO   
219528             IF IRP64-PAX-SEQ-NUM <= ZERO 
219528*               COMPUTE WS-MAR-AMT = MAR-ACTUAL-QUANTITY *
219528                COMPUTE WS-MAR-AMT = IRP64-ACTUAL-QTY *   
219528*                                          MAR-ACTUAL-AMOUNT  
219528                                     IRP64-ACTUAL-AMOUNT  
219528*               IF  MAR-PEN-XFR-AMT < WS-MAR-AMT  
219528                IF  IRP64-PEN-XFR-AMT < WS-MAR-AMT
CCLAUS                    COMPUTE WS-IBR-PEN-GST-AMT ROUNDED =  
CCLAUS                        WS-IBR-PEN-GST-AMT  + 
219528*                    ((MAR-PEN-XFR-AMT / MAR-PRC-XCHG-RATE) * 
219528                     ((IRP64-PEN-XFR-AMT / IRP64-PRC-XCHG-RATE) * 
CCLAUS                     RSP06-IB-EFF-INCL-TRNS-GST-PCT OF RSP06-RGC) 
CCLAUS                    COMPUTE WS-IBR-PEN-GST-AMT ROUNDED =  
CCLAUS                            WS-IBR-PEN-GST-AMT +  
219528*                   (((WS-MAR-AMT - MAR-PEN-XFR-AMT) /
219528*                                         MAR-PRC-XCHG-RATE) *
219528                    (((WS-MAR-AMT - IRP64-PEN-XFR-AMT) /  
219528                                          IRP64-PRC-XCHG-RATE) *  
CCLAUS                     RSP06-IB-EFF-INCL-GST-PCT OF RSP06-RGC)  
CCLAUS                ELSE  
CCLAUS                    COMPUTE WS-IBR-PEN-GST-AMT ROUNDED =  
CCLAUS                        WS-IBR-PEN-GST-AMT  + 
219528*                     ((WS-MAR-AMT / MAR-PRC-XCHG-RATE) * 
219528                      ((WS-MAR-AMT / IRP64-PRC-XCHG-RATE) *   
CCLAUS                     RSP06-IB-EFF-INCL-GST-PCT OF RSP06-RGC)  
CCLAUS                END-IF
CCLAUS                    COMPUTE WS-IBR-PEN-COMM-GST-AMT ROUNDED = 
CCLAUS                     WS-IBR-PEN-COMM-GST-AMT +
219528*                        ((MAR-COMM-AMT / MAR-PRC-XCHG-RATE) *
219528                         ((IRP64-COMM-AMT / IRP64-PRC-XCHG-RATE) *
CCLAUS                     RSP06-IB-EFF-EXCL-GST-PCT OF RSP06-RGC)  
CCLAUS            END-IF
108267        WHEN 'N'  
108267             COMPUTE RSP06-IBR-NSF-AMT OF RSP06-RGC ROUNDED = 
108267                     RSP06-IBR-NSF-AMT OF RSP06-RGC + 
219528*                    (MAR-ACTUAL-QUANTITY * MAR-ACTUAL-AMOUNT /   
219528*                     MAR-PRC-XCHG-RATE)  
219528                     (IRP64-ACTUAL-QTY * IRP64-ACTUAL-AMOUNT /
219528                      IRP64-PRC-XCHG-RATE)
108267        WHEN 'A'  
108267             COMPUTE RSP06-IBR-ADMIN-FEE-AMT OF RSP06-RGC ROUNDED 
108267                     = RSP06-IBR-ADMIN-FEE-AMT OF RSP06-RGC + 
219528*                    (MAR-ACTUAL-QUANTITY * MAR-ACTUAL-AMOUNT /   
219528*                     MAR-PRC-XCHG-RATE)  
219528                     (IRP64-ACTUAL-QTY * IRP64-ACTUAL-AMOUNT /
219528                      IRP64-PRC-XCHG-RATE)
219528*            IF MAR-PAX-SEQ-NUM <= ZERO   
219528             IF IRP64-PAX-SEQ-NUM <= ZERO 
CCLAUS                 COMPUTE WS-IBR-ADMIN-FEE-GST-AMT ROUNDED =   
CCLAUS                   WS-IBR-ADMIN-FEE-GST-AMT + 
219528*                 ((MAR-ACTUAL-QUANTITY * MAR-ACTUAL-AMOUNT / 
219528*                                      MAR-PRC-XCHG-RATE) *   
219528                  ((IRP64-ACTUAL-QTY * IRP64-ACTUAL-AMOUNT /  
219528                   IRP64-PRC-XCHG-RATE) * 
CCLAUS                     RSP06-IB-EFF-INCL-GST-PCT OF RSP06-RGC)  
CCLAUS             END-IF   
108267        WHEN 'C'  
108267             COMPUTE RSP06-IBR-CREDIT-AMT OF RSP06-RGC ROUNDED =  
108267                     RSP06-IBR-CREDIT-AMT OF RSP06-RGC +  
219528*                    (MAR-ACTUAL-QUANTITY * MAR-ACTUAL-AMOUNT /   
219528*                     MAR-PRC-XCHG-RATE)  
219528                     (IRP64-ACTUAL-QTY * IRP64-ACTUAL-AMOUNT /
219528                      IRP64-PRC-XCHG-RATE)
219528*            IF MAR-PAX-SEQ-NUM <= ZERO   
219528             IF IRP64-PAX-SEQ-NUM <= ZERO 
CCLAUS                 COMPUTE WS-IBR-CREDIT-GST-AMT ROUNDED =  
CCLAUS                   WS-IBR-CREDIT-GST-AMT +
219528*                 ((MAR-ACTUAL-QUANTITY * MAR-ACTUAL-AMOUNT / 
219528*                                      MAR-PRC-XCHG-RATE) *   
219528                  ((IRP64-ACTUAL-QTY * IRP64-ACTUAL-AMOUNT /  
219528                   IRP64-PRC-XCHG-RATE) * 
CCLAUS                     RSP06-IB-EFF-INCL-GST-PCT OF RSP06-RGC)  
CCLAUS             END-IF   
108267        WHEN 'D'  
108267             COMPUTE RSP06-IBR-MISC-AMT OF RSP06-RGC ROUNDED =
108267                     RSP06-IBR-MISC-AMT OF RSP06-RGC +
219528*                    (MAR-ACTUAL-QUANTITY * MAR-ACTUAL-AMOUNT /   
219528*                     MAR-PRC-XCHG-RATE)  
219528                     (IRP64-ACTUAL-QTY * IRP64-ACTUAL-AMOUNT /
219528                      IRP64-PRC-XCHG-RATE)
219528*            IF MAR-PAX-SEQ-NUM <= ZERO   
219528             IF IRP64-PAX-SEQ-NUM <= ZERO 
CCLAUS                 COMPUTE WS-IBR-MISC-GST-AMT ROUNDED =
CCLAUS                   WS-IBR-MISC-GST-AMT +  
219528*                 ((MAR-ACTUAL-QUANTITY * MAR-ACTUAL-AMOUNT / 
219528*                                      MAR-PRC-XCHG-RATE) *   
219528                  ((IRP64-ACTUAL-QTY * IRP64-ACTUAL-AMOUNT /  
219528                   IRP64-PRC-XCHG-RATE) * 
CCLAUS                     RSP06-IB-EFF-INCL-GST-PCT OF RSP06-RGC)  
CCLAUS             END-IF   
CCLAUS*       WHEN 'T'  
CCLAUS*            COMPUTE RSP06-IBR-MISC-AIR-TKT-AMT OF RSP06-RGC  
CCLAUS*                    ROUNDED =
CCLAUS*                    RSP06-IBR-MISC-AIR-TKT-AMT OF RSP06-RGC +
CCLAUS*                    (MAR-ACTUAL-QUANTITY * MAR-ACTUAL-AMOUNT /   
CCLAUS*                     MAR-PRC-XCHG-RATE)  
108267     END-EVALUATE.

PPC470         RSP06-PDI-CRUISE-COMM-AMT  OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB).  

           IF RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB) > SPACES AND
                 (RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB) NOT =   
                  RSP06-PAX-DINING-CNF OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB)) 
              MOVE RSP06-PAX-DINING-PREF OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB) TO 
                   RSP06-PDI-DINING-DISP-CODE OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB)   
              MOVE 'WAITLISTED' TO  
                    RSP06-PDI-DINING-STATUS OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB) 
           ELSE 
              IF RSP06-PAX-DINING-CNF OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB) > SPACES AND  
                    (RSP06-PAX-DINING-CNF OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB) NOT = 'U')
                 MOVE RSP06-PAX-DINING-CNF OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB) TO

                 MOVE 'CONFIRMED' TO
                       RSP06-PDI-DINING-STATUS OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB)  
              ELSE

                      RSP06-PDI-DINING-DISP-CODE OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB)
                 MOVE SPACES TO 
                       RSP06-PDI-DINING-STATUS OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB). 

           IF RSP06-CSSXR-EXIST-IND OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB) = 'Y'   
              IF (RSP06-CSSXR-CAR-PAX-BKG-NUM OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB)   
                    > SPACES AND

                    > ZEROES)  OR

                 MOVE 'Y'  TO RSP06-PDI-ONACCT-FLAG OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB).

                RSP06-PDI-REQUEST-STATUS OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB).   

           EVALUATE RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-DISP-CODE OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB)  
              WHEN 'C'  
                 MOVE RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-AMT OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB) TO

             WHEN 'A'

                      RSP06-PDI-PACKAGE-AMT OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB) 
                 MOVE RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-COMM-AMT OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB) TO  
                      RSP06-PDI-PACKAGE-COMM-AMT OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB)
             WHEN 'I'

                      RSP06-PDI-INSURANCE-AMT OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB)   
                 MOVE RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-COMM-AMT OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB) TO  
                      RSP06-PDI-INSURANCE-COMM-AMT OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB)  
             WHEN 'T'

                      RSP06-PDI-TRANSFER-AMT OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB)
                 ADD RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-COMM-AMT OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB) TO   
                      RSP06-PDI-TRANSFER-COMM-AMT OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB)   
           END-EVALUATE.

           EVALUATE RSP06-PAX-ADDON-DISP-CODE OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB)   
              WHEN 'C'  
                 ADD RSP06-PAX-ADDON-AMT OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB) TO 
                      RSP06-PDI-CRUISE-AMT OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB)  
                 ADD RSP06-PAX-ADDON-COMM-AMT OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB) TO
                      RSP06-PDI-CRUISE-COMM-AMT OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB) 
             WHEN 'A'

                      RSP06-PDI-ADDON-AMT OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB)   
                 ADD RSP06-PAX-ADDON-COMM-AMT OF RSP06-RGC (PSUB) TO

CCLAUS  
CCLAUS     EVALUATE IBR-BOOK-NUM (6:1)  
CCLAUS        WHEN 'B' WHEN 'C' WHEN '0'
CCLAUS              MOVE '0'  TO INDV-BOOKING-CALCAREA (9:1)
CCLAUS        WHEN 'D' WHEN 'F' WHEN '1'
CCLAUS              MOVE '1'  TO INDV-BOOKING-CALCAREA (9:1)
CCLAUS        WHEN 'G' WHEN 'H' WHEN '2'
CCLAUS              MOVE '2'  TO INDV-BOOKING-CALCAREA (9:1)
CCLAUS        WHEN 'J' WHEN 'K' WHEN '3'
CCLAUS              MOVE '3'  TO INDV-BOOKING-CALCAREA (9:1)
CCLAUS        WHEN 'L' WHEN 'M' WHEN '4'
CCLAUS              MOVE '4'  TO INDV-BOOKING-CALCAREA (9:1)
CCLAUS        WHEN 'N' WHEN 'P' WHEN '5'
CCLAUS              MOVE '5'  TO INDV-BOOKING-CALCAREA (9:1)
CCLAUS        WHEN 'Q' WHEN 'R' WHEN '6'
CCLAUS              MOVE '6'  TO INDV-BOOKING-CALCAREA (9:1)
CCLAUS        WHEN 'S' WHEN 'T' WHEN '7'
CCLAUS              MOVE '7'  TO INDV-BOOKING-CALCAREA (9:1)
CCLAUS        WHEN 'V' WHEN 'W' WHEN '8'
CCLAUS              MOVE '8'  TO INDV-BOOKING-CALCAREA (9:1)
CCLAUS        WHEN 'X' WHEN 'Z' WHEN '9'
CCLAUS              MOVE '9'  TO INDV-BOOKING-CALCAREA (9:1)
CCLAUS     END-EVALUATE.
